How is the `neural` data filtered based on quality controls?\n114:i. Only clusters whose `label` is 1 are kept. The label is a score of 0, 1/3, 2/3 or 1 that the IBL pipeline assigns from a set of spike sorting metrics, so requiring 1 keeps the units that pass all of them. Across the release this keeps 75,708 of 621,733 clusters. Perform all of the following checks:\\n\\n**Check 1: Accuracy vs chance analysis**\\n- For each output, compare validation accuracy to chance (1/num_classes)\\n- Accuracy below chance indicates a bug, investigate and fix conversion issues to improve this. \\n- Accuracy below 1.5x chance may also indicate a bug, investigate whether changes in conversion logic could improve accuracy. \\n\\n**Check 2: Accuracy comparison to papers**\\n- Find every decoding accuracy reported in the reference papers\\n- Create a table comparing your accuracy to the papers's accuracy for each variable\\n- If your accuracy is lower than the reported accuracy, this may indicate a bug in your conversion that you must fix. \\n- Differences in algorithm/architecture should be relatively small because of the data dimensionality and size. Do not use this as an excuse to dismiss differences, investigate them completely before moving on. \\n\\n**Check 3: Train vs validation gap**\\n- If training accuracy is much higher (>1.5x) than validation accuracy for any output, investigate overfitting or data leakage. \\n\\n**If accuracy is low for an output, try these specific debugging steps:**\\n1. Verify the output values are correct by loading raw data and checking 3 specific trials\\n2. Check temporal alignment \\u2014 plot neural + output for a single trial to verify they're synchronized\\n3. Check whether the output has enough variation (not 99% one class)\\n4. Check that filtering steps for neural activity streams are followed. \\n5. Check that processing of the data matches the reference code and papers. \\n\\n**Iteration protocol**: If ANY check above reveals an issue:\\n1. Fix the issue in convert_data.py\\n2. Document each iteration: what was found, what was fixed, what the re-check showed\\n\\n**Report all checks and results** to `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` to convince the user that the conversion and decoding has been done correctly. \\n\\n**Done when**: You have documented your review findings and all issues are resolved in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \\\"Step 12\\\".\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Step 13: Documentation and Cleanup\\n\\n**Goal**: Finalize documentation and organize files.\\n\\n**Actions**:\\n- Ensure CONVERSION_NOTES.md is complete with all:\\n  - Key decisions and rationale\\n  - Checks for correctness of the conversion\\n  - Validation results and tables\\n  - Any issues found and how they were resolved\\n- Create a user-facing `README.md` summarizing:\\n  - Dataset description\\n  - How to load and use the converted data\\n  - Output format specification\\n  - Key statistics\\n- Move analysis/investigation scripts to a `cache/` folder\\n- Create `cache/README_CACHE.md` documenting cached files\\n\\n**Done when**: README.md exists, CONVERSION_NOTES.md is complete, and directory is clean.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## CONVERSION_NOTES.md Template\\n\\n**Create this file IMMEDIATELY in Step 0. 0 is a no response, dropped\n    61\tPRIOR = {0.2: 0, 0.5: 1, 0.8: 2}\n    62\tMOVEMENT = ['low', 'medium', 'high']\n    63\t\n    64\tINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block']\n    65\tOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\n    66\tOUTPUT_VALUES = [['left', 'right'], ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'], MOVEMENT, MOVEMENT]\n    67\t\n    68\t# the cache location is not known until --datadir is parsed, and the connection cannot be\n    69\t# shared across a fork anyway, so connect() fills this in per process rather than at import\n    70\tone = None\n    71\tsubset_csv = None    # DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv path, or None on the full dataset; The metadata that resolution needs is cached under one_cache/.rest, so a run\n    83\t    does not have to reach Alyx.\n    84\t\n    85\t    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder.\n    86\t    Sets the globals `one` and `subset_csv`; returns nothing.\n    87\t    \"\"\"\n    88\t    global one\n    89\t    global subset_csv\n    90\t    cache = os.path.join(datadir, ONE_CACHE_DIRNAME)\n    91\t    tables = os.path.join(cache, RELEASE)\n    92\t    client = dict(base_url=BASE_URL, silent=True, cache_dir=cache, tables_dir=tables,\n    93\t                  mode='remote')\n    94\t    try:\n    95\t        # No password: passing one forces re-authentication over the network, while the\n    96\t        # token the fetch cached needs no connection.\n    97\t        one = ONE(**client)\n    98\t    except Exception:\n    99\t        one = ONE(password=PASSWORD, **client)\n   100\t    one.alyx.default_expiry = REST_EXPIRY\n   101\t    # One.load_cache reads the parquet tables where they are; the OneAlyx override would\n   102\t    # ask Alyx for a newer copy first. The ONE\n   126\t    # release index is unmodified and still lists every released session, so\n   127\t    # restrict to the eids named there -- otherwise a session that is not on disk\n   128\t    # would be fetched over the network. probabilityLeft is constant within a block, so a change of value starts a new block\n   161\t    block = (trials.probabilityLeft != trials.probabilityLeft.shift()).cumsum()\n   162\t\n   163\t    return pd.DataFrame({'choice': trials.choice.map(CHOICE),\n   164\t                         'prior': trials.probabilityLeft.map(PRIOR),\n   165\t                         'trial_in_block': trials.groupby(block).cumcount()})\n   166\t\n   167\t\n   168\tdef covered(times, stim_on):\n   169\t    \"\"\"Trials whose window is spanned by this stream, allowing the one bin gap the reference allows.\"\"\"\n   170\t    start, stop = stim_on + T_START, stim_on + T_STOP\n   171\t    begin = np.searchsorted(times, start, side='right')      # first sample inside the window\n   172\t    end = np.searchsorted(times, stop, side='left') - 1       # last sample inside the window\n   173\t\n   174\t    # a trial with no sample at all, or a gap at either edge, has no usable trace\n   175\t    inside = begin <= end\n   176\t    first = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(begin, 0, times.size - 1)], np.inf)\n   177\t    last = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(end, 0, times.size - 1)], -np.inf)\n   178\t    return inside & (np.abs(start - first) <= BIN) & (np.abs(stop - last) <= BIN)\n   179\t\n   180\t\n   181\tdef usable_trials(trials, wheel_times, camera_times):\n   182\t    \"\"\"Trials the paper would keep, that also have a full wheel and camera window.\"\"\"\n   183\t    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()\n   184\t    reaction = trials.firstMovement_times.to_numpy() - stim_on\n   185\t\n   186\t    keep = (reaction >= MIN_RT) & (reaction <= MAX_RT)          #
